### **What Went Wrong?**

1. **Guessing "wind" and "rain" repeatedly**:
   - These words are **not on the board**.
   - The game only allows guessing **words that are on the board**.
   - This led to repeated invalid guesses and wasted turns.

   - This caused the Blue team to **lose the game**.

---

### **What Could Have Been Done Differently?**

1. **Review the board** more carefully and look for **synonyms or associations** with "storm" that **are on the board**.
   - For example:
     - **Change** – a storm can bring change.
     - **War** – a metaphorical storm.
     - **Cut** – could be a storm’s effect on a tree or something else.
     - **Knot** – not clearly related, but worth considering.

2. **Ask the Spymaster for clarification** if unsure, though in Codenames, the Spymaster can only give one clue per turn.

3. **Pass** if unsure, to avoid losing the game through invalid guesses.

---

### **Final Thoughts**

As the Blue Operative, it’s crucial to:
- **Stay focused** on the clue and the board.
- **Avoid guessing words not on the board**.
- **Avoid guessing Red, Neutral, or Assassin words**.
- **Be strategic** with guesses, especially when only a few remain.

   - If unsure, the Operative could ask the Spymaster for clarification (though in most Codenames setups, the Spymaster only gives one clue per turn and cannot clarify).

3. **Use the Pass Option**:
   - If uncertain, it's safer to **pass** than to guess incorrectly and lose the game.
